# Scooter build history — exact preservation manifest

Prepared 2026-11-20. **1,786 files; 3,535,732,227 logical bytes (3.536 GB; 3.293 GiB).** Sizes are content bytes, not allocation or predicted free-space savings. Paths are unique; equal-content copies count separately when retained inside explicit permanent packages.

## Scope

This is a preserve-before-deletion list, not a deletion allowlist or proof that backup exists. Before removing an October 5/6/7 parent, retain every listed descendant at a surviving durable destination, verify size/SHA256 and record its source-to-destination map. Leaving it inside a deleted parent does not preserve it. No LFS operation, off-device backup, fresh-checkout fetch, native-open check, deletion or commit was performed. Only bounded repo PNG copies were created.

Preserve the [ledger](scooter-build-history.md), [image folder](scooter-build-history/) and this manifest as ordinary source. The ledger/PNGs are listed below; the manifest's own self-hash is excluded to avoid recursion. Unrelated character/rider paths are carried forward without expanding scooter-cleanup authority.

## Cross-check with audit Section 6

All **17 of 17** canonical binary paths are included, with the same **598,348,990 bytes**. These include accepted v012, footwell V028, V031 and wheel reference, character finals, actual v052 rig/poses, helmet gold and pose reference. Stopped v002 is a source/reference, not an approved pose.

This selection is larger than minimal LFS because it protects history before parent removal. Additions: eight protected original sources; three additional historically accepted part natives; full V031 recovery/sign-off; four explicitly permanent reconstruction scopes; six retained gzip representatives/restore maps; authority images; selected original renders; cited records; small recovery-era Markdown/Python recipes; and P92/P95/P98 terminal witnesses pending the unresolved finish lineage. These additions are **not a proposal to upload everything to LFS**. Normal-text companions stay normal text; do not import the entire recovery archive.

Omission from minimal LFS never authorized deleting eight protected originals. Existing four-scope/six-representative retention is carried forward. P98's 333 MB build record preserves inspected terminal parent/limit fields; its giant observed-state subtree was not semantically reviewed. Recipes/prose may be inventoried without content review or proof of dependency completeness. The ledger names the records actually supporting claims.

The audit already checked historical protected digests and all 52 V031 manifest entries. Current hashes below were calculated for this concrete selection and chosen stage witnesses, not another archive-wide audit. Character source ZIP/autosaves/candidates and unrelated roots retain existing audit holds outside this task.

## Totals

- All unique paths below: **1,786 files, 3,535,732,227 bytes**.
- Audit Section 6 subset: 17 files, 598,348,990 bytes.
- Eight protected-source subset: 959,895,601 bytes.
- Six gzip representatives: 789,488,682 bytes.
- New fallback PNG folder: 28 files, 14,967,775 bytes (14.97 MB), below approximately 15 MB.
- Subsets overlap. Do not sum bullets. This manifest's own bytes are excluded.
